== S. The stance sweep (P2b.2b.2c.2d design §3.5): the AC stop and the drive as Gugan 5's strokes on the default world (guganSetup: Gugan's mallet, the default gap 0.7500 mm, 3.0000 m/s contact speed by strokeForContact, the drive at guideEffort 0, the stop at jab 0, ON_TIME, each preset's own drive), leans −8° to +4° in 1° steps set through handsAhead at the preset's top hand and up, the AC stop also at up -25.0000 mm for −7° and −8°; each at the stroke's own grips and at the other's (bottom, gripTension, bottomGrip). A lean whose head's lowest point at contact lies in the turf (turfLimited, mm) is printed turf-limited and not played. Per run: the ratio against its band (* within), (v₂/w₁)², the routes, the head's least clearance from the turf over the strike window (15.0000 ms from first touch, mm) and any turf contact; per stroke and grip set, the lean nearest the target and the ratio's rate per degree; then the canonical drive across the leans ==
